# Lifespan audit: stops dated outside their subject's own birth-death span

119 stop(s) dated before birth, 2026 dated after death (plus 363 more after-death stops already excluded as legacy/afterlife-labeled segments), out of 37942 total stops (5746 subjects skipped: no parseable or too-hedged years span).

Caveat, confirmed by hand on the two cases that started this script: most 'before birth' stops are deliberate ancestry/family-background narration, and most remaining 'after death' stops are deliberate legacy/reception narration that just didn't happen to use one of the segment-name keywords this script filters on, or a thematic pin borrowed from another subject's real stop. This list is a set of candidates to read by eye, not a bug list.
